    59	#define MPI_CHECK(call) checkMpi((call), #call)
    60	#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call)
    61	
    62	void fill(double* values, const index_t count, const double maxVal) {
    63	    for (index_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    64	        values[i] = maxVal * (std::rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    65	    }
    66	}
    67	
    68	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t nnz,
    69	                      const index_t dim) {
    70	    index_t assigned = 0;
    71	    const double probability = static_cast<double>(nnz) /
    72	                               (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
    73	
    74	    std::srand(8675309);
    75	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    76	    const std::uint64_t totalEntries = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(dim) * dim;
    77	    for (index_t row = 0; row < dim; ++row) {
    78	        rowDelimiters[row] = assigned;
    79	        for (index_t col = 0; col < dim; ++col) {
    80	            const std::uint64_t position = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dim + col;
    81	            const std::uint64_t entriesLeft = totalEntries - position;
    82	            const std::uint64_t needToAssign = nnz - assigned;
    83	            if (entriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
    84	                fillRemaining = true;
    85	            }
    86	            const double randomValue = static_cast<double>(std::rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    87	            if ((assigned < nnz && randomValue <= probability) || fillRemaining) {
    88	                cols[assigned++] = col;
    89	            }
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	    rowDelimiters[dim] = nnz;
    93	}
    94	
    95	// The outer loop is independent; the inner loop intentionally retains CSR order so
    96	// that the OpenMP reference has the same floating-point evaluation order per row.
    97	void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
    98	             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    99	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   100	    for (std::int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<std::int64_t>(dim); ++row) {
   101	        double sum = 0.0;
   102	        for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
   103	            sum += val[item] * vec[cols[item]];
   104	        }
   105	        out[row] = sum;
   106	    }
   107	}
   108	
   109	__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   110	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   111	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   112	                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
   113	                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
   114	    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   115	    if (row >= rows) {
   116	        return;
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    double sum = 0.0;
   120	    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
   121	        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
   122	    }
   123	    out[row] = sum;
   124	}
   125	
   126	__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   127	                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   128	                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   129	                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
   130	                               double* __restrict__ out) {
   131	    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
   132	    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
   133	    if (row >= rows) {
   134	        return;
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    double sum = 0.0;
   138	    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   139	         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
   140	        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
   141	    }
   142	
   143	#pragma unroll
   144	    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   145	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   146	    }
   147	    if (lane == 0) {
   148	        out[row] = sum;
   149	    }
   150	}
   151	
   152	void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
   153	                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const index_t nnz, double* dOut,
   154	                cudaStream_t stream) {
   155	    if (rows == 0) {
   156	        return;
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    const double averageRowLength = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
   160	    if (averageRowLength <= 16.0) {
   161	        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
   162	        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
   163	                                                                 rows, dOut);
   164	    } else {
   165	        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
   166	        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
   167	                                                               rows, dOut);
   168	    }
   169	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   170	}
   171	
   172	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
   173	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   174	        const double ref = reference[i];
   175	        const double res = result[i];
   176	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
   177	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   178	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i,
   179	                            ref, res);
   180	                return false;
   181	            }
   182	        } else {
   183	            const double relativeError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
   184	            if (relativeError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   185	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e "
   186	                            "(rel error: %.10e)\n",
   187	                            i, ref, res, relativeError);
   188	                return false;
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	    }
   192	    return true;
   193	}
   194	
   195	void printUsage(const char* program) {
   196	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
   197	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   198	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   199	    std::printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   200	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   201	    std::printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   202	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   203	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   204	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   205	}
   206	
   207	bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
   208	    errno = 0;
   209	    char* end = nullptr;
   210	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   211	    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
   212	        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   213	        return false;
   214	    }
   215	    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
   216	    return true;
   217	}
   218	
   219	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   220	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   221	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel));
   222	
   223	    int worldRank = 0;
   224	    int worldSize = 1;
   225	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank));
   226	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize));
   227	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   228	        abortMpi("MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    index_t numRows = 1024;
   232	    index_t sparsity = 10;
   233	    index_t iterations = 10;
   234	    double maxVal = 1.0;
   235	    bool validate = false;
   236	    bool printResults = false;
   237	    bool showHelp = false;
   238	    bool argumentsValid = true;
   239	
   240	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   241	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   242	            argumentsValid = parseIndex(argv[++i], &numRows) && argumentsValid;
   243	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   244	            argumentsValid = parseIndex(argv[++i], &sparsity) && argumentsValid;
   245	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   246	            argumentsValid = parseIndex(argv[++i], &iterations) && argumentsValid;
   247	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   248	            char* end = nullptr;
   249	            maxVal = std::strtod(argv[++i], &end);
   250	            argumentsValid = end != nullptr && *end == '\0' && std::isfinite(maxVal) &&
   251	                             argumentsValid;
   252	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   253	            validate = true;
   254	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   255	            printResults = true;
   256	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   257	            showHelp = true;
   258	        } else {
   259	            argumentsValid = false;
   260	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   261	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unknown or incomplete option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   262	            }
   263	        }
   264	    }
   265	
   266	    if (showHelp) {
   267	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   268	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   269	        }
   270	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   271	        return 0;
   272	    }
   273	    if (!argumentsValid || numRows == 0 || sparsity == 0 || iterations == 0) {
   274	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   275	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size, sparsity, and iteration count must be positive.\n");
   276	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   277	        }
   278	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   279	        return 1;
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    const std::uint64_t matrixEntries = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
   283	    const std::uint64_t nnz64 = matrixEntries / sparsity;
   284	    if (numRows > static_cast<index_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   285	        nnz64 > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   286	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   287	            std::fprintf(stderr,
   288	                         "This MPI build supports at most INT_MAX rows and nonzero entries.\n");
   289	        }
   290	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   291	        return 1;
   292	    }
   293	    const index_t nItems = static_cast<index_t>(nnz64);
   294	
   295	    MPI_Comm localComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   296	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank,
   297	                                  MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm));
   298	    int localRank = 0;
   299	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank));
   300	
   301	    int deviceCount = 0;
   302	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
   303	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   304	        abortMpi("The hybrid benchmark requires at least one CUDA GPU per node");
   305	    }
   306	    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
   307	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   308	    cudaDeviceProp deviceProperties{};
   309	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&deviceProperties, device));
   310	
   311	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   312	        std::printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   313	        std::printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   314	        std::printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
   315	        std::printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", nItems,
   316	                    100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / matrixEntries));
   317	        std::printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
   318	        std::printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
   319	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   320	        std::printf("Hybrid execution: %d MPI rank(s), up to %d OpenMP thread(s)/rank, "
   321	                    "%d CUDA device(s)/node\n",
   322	                    worldSize, omp_get_max_threads(), deviceCount);
   323	        std::printf("Rank 0 CUDA device: %s\n", deviceProperties.name);
   324	        std::printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    std::vector<double> values;
   328	    std::vector<index_t> columns;
   329	    std::vector<index_t> rowDelimiters;
   330	    std::vector<double> vector(numRows);
   331	    std::vector<double> output;
   332	    std::vector<double> reference;
   333	
   334	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   335	        values.resize(nItems);
   336	        columns.resize(nItems);
   337	        rowDelimiters.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(numRows) + 1);
   338	        output.resize(numRows);
   339	        fill(vector.data(), numRows, maxVal);
   340	        fill(values.data(), nItems, maxVal);
   341	        initRandomMatrix(columns.data(), rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);
   342	
   343	        if (validate) {
   344	            std::printf("Computing OpenMP reference solution...\n");
   345	            reference.resize(numRows);
   346	            spmvCpu(values.data(), columns.data(), rowDelimiters.data(), vector.data(),
   347	                    numRows, reference.data());
   348	        }
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(vector.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   352	                        MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   353	
   354	    std::vector<int> rowStarts;
   355	    std::vector<int> rowCounts;
   356	    std::vector<int> nnzStarts;
   357	    std::vector<int> nnzCounts;
   358	    std::vector<int> delimiterCounts;
   359	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   360	        rowStarts.resize(worldSize + 1);
   361	        rowCounts.resize(worldSize);
   362	        nnzStarts.resize(worldSize);
   363	        nnzCounts.resize(worldSize);
   364	        delimiterCounts.resize(worldSize);
   365	        rowStarts[0] = 0;
   366	        rowStarts[worldSize] = static_cast<int>(numRows);
   367	        for (int rank = 1; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
   368	            const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>(
   369	                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nItems) * rank) / worldSize);
   370	            const auto boundary = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters.begin(),
   371	                                                   rowDelimiters.end(), target);
   372	            rowStarts[rank] = static_cast<int>(boundary - rowDelimiters.begin());
   373	        }
   374	        for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
   375	            rowCounts[rank] = rowStarts[rank + 1] - rowStarts[rank];
   376	            nnzStarts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rowDelimiters[rowStarts[rank]]);
   377	            nnzCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rowDelimiters[rowStarts[rank + 1]]) -
   378	                              nnzStarts[rank];
   379	            delimiterCounts[rank] = rowCounts[rank] + 1;
   380	        }
   381	    }
   382	
   383	    int localRowStart = 0;
   384	    int localRows = 0;
   385	    int localNnzStart = 0;
   386	    int localNnz = 0;
   387	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatter(rowStarts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, &localRowStart, 1, MPI_INT, 0,
   388	                          MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   389	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatter(rowCounts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, &localRows, 1, MPI_INT, 0,
   390	                          MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   391	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatter(nnzStarts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, &localNnzStart, 1, MPI_INT, 0,
   392	                          MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   393	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatter(nnzCounts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, &localNnz, 1, MPI_INT, 0,
   394	                          MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   395	
   396	    std::vector<double> localValues(localNnz);
   397	    std::vector<index_t> localColumns(localNnz);
   398	    std::vector<index_t> localRowDelimiters(static_cast<std::size_t>(localRows) + 1);
   399	    std::vector<double> localOutput(localRows);
   400	
   401	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatterv(values.data(), nnzCounts.data(), nnzStarts.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
   402	                           localValues.data(), localNnz, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   403	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatterv(columns.data(), nnzCounts.data(), nnzStarts.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,
   404	                           localColumns.data(), localNnz, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   405	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatterv(rowDelimiters.data(), delimiterCounts.data(), rowStarts.data(),
   406	                           MPI_UINT32_T, localRowDelimiters.data(), localRows + 1,
   407	                           MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   408	
   409	    // Rebase and first-touch the rank-local CSR storage in parallel on the host.
   410	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   411	    for (std::int64_t row = 0; row <= localRows; ++row) {
   412	        localRowDelimiters[row] -= static_cast<index_t>(localNnzStart);
   413	        if (row < localRows) {
   414	            localOutput[row] = 0.0;
   415	        }
   416	    }
   417	
   418	    double* dValues = nullptr;
   419	    index_t* dColumns = nullptr;
   420	    index_t* dRowDelimiters = nullptr;
   421	    double* dVector = nullptr;
   422	    double* dOutput = nullptr;
   423	    const std::size_t allocatedNnz = std::max<std::size_t>(1, localValues.size());
   424	    const std::size_t allocatedRows = std::max<std::size_t>(1, localOutput.size());
   425	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dValues), allocatedNnz * sizeof(double)));
   426	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dColumns), allocatedNnz * sizeof(index_t)));
   427	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dRowDelimiters),
   428	                          localRowDelimiters.size() * sizeof(index_t)));
   429	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dVector),
   430	                          static_cast<std::size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double)));
   431	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dOutput), allocatedRows * sizeof(double)));
   432	
   433	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   434	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   435	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dValues, localValues.data(), localValues.size() * sizeof(double),
   436	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   437	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dColumns, localColumns.data(),
   438	                               localColumns.size() * sizeof(index_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice,
   439	                               stream));
   440	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dRowDelimiters, localRowDelimiters.data(),
   441	                               localRowDelimiters.size() * sizeof(index_t),
   442	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   443	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dVector, vector.data(),
   444	                               static_cast<std::size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   445	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   446	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   447	
   448	    // Warm up the context and selected kernel before the measured region.
   449	    launchSpmv(dValues, dColumns, dRowDelimiters, dVector,
   450	               static_cast<index_t>(localRows), static_cast<index_t>(localNnz), dOutput, stream);
   451	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   452	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   453	
   454	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   455	        std::printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   456	    }
   457	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   458	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   459	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   460	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   461	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream));
   462	    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
   463	        launchSpmv(dValues, dColumns, dRowDelimiters, dVector,
   464	                   static_cast<index_t>(localRows), static_cast<index_t>(localNnz), dOutput,
   465	                   stream);
   466	    }
   467	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream));
   468	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   469	
   470	    float localElapsedMs = 0.0F;
   471	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localElapsedMs, startEvent, stopEvent));
   472	    float elapsedMs = 0.0F;
   473	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localElapsedMs, &elapsedMs, 1, MPI_FLOAT, MPI_MAX, 0,
   474	                         MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   475	
   476	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localOutput.data(), dOutput,
   477	                               localOutput.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
   478	                               stream));
   479	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   480	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(localOutput.data(), localRows, MPI_DOUBLE, output.data(),
   481	                          rowCounts.data(), rowStarts.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   482	
   483	    int result = 0;
   484	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   485	        const double seconds = static_cast<double>(elapsedMs) / 1000.0;
   486	        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(nItems) * iterations) / seconds / 1e9;
   487	        const double averageMs = elapsedMs / iterations;
   488	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedMs);
   489	        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", averageMs);
   490	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   491	
   492	        if (printResults) {
   493	            print_results(output, "OutputVector");
   494	        }
   495	        if (validate) {
   496	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   497	            const bool valid = verifyResults(reference.data(), output.data(), numRows);
   498	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   499	            result = valid ? 0 : 1;
   500	        }
   501	    }
   502	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   503	
   504	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   505	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   506	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   507	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dValues));
   508	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dColumns));
   509	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dRowDelimiters));
   510	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dVector));
   511	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dOutput));
   512	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localComm));
   513	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   514	    return result;
   515	}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: spmvCpu
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
-//

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
     }
+    out[row] = sum;
 }
 
-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector
-//
-// ****************************************************************************
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const index_t nnz, double* dOut,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageRowLength = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 16.0) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

-// ****************************************************************************

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

-// ****************************************************************************

+// The outer loop is independent; the inner loop intentionally retains CSR order so

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+        return;

+    char error[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+    int length = 0;

+    std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed: %.*s", operation, length, error);

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

-// Arguments:

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// ****************************************************************************

-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA SpMV benchmark.

Key changes:

- Nonzero-balanced CSR row distribution across MPI ranks.
- Rank-local CUDA device assignment and adaptive thread/warp SpMV kernels.
- CUDA Graph execution to reduce iteration launch overhead.
- OpenMP-parallel host preprocessing and CPU reference calculation.
- MPI broadcast/scatter/gather with timing based on the slowest rank.
- Preserved executable name, CLI, deterministic initialization, validation, and result reporting.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r5/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1) for mandatory MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA.
- Modified only [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp:1) and `CMakeLists.txt`.

Validation completed successfully for:

- Single and four MPI ranks
- Both adaptive CUDA kernel paths
- Default-size matrix
- Empty matrices and ranks with no assigned rows
- Matching result hashes between single- and multi-rank executions

For deployment, use one MPI rank per GPU and set `OMP_NUM_THREADS` to the CPU cores allocated to each rank.

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

+__global__ void spmvThreadKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+    out[row] = sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rows,
+                               double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (CUDA_WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / CUDA_WARP_SIZE;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += CUDA_WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += val[item] * __ldg(vec + cols[item]);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = CUDA_WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* dVal, const index_t* dCols, const index_t* dRows,
+                const double* dVec, const index_t rows, const bool useWarpKernel,
+                double* dOut, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (useWarpKernel) {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                               rows, dOut);
+    } else {
+        const unsigned int blocks = (rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        spmvThreadKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(dVal, dCols, dRows, dVec,
+                                                                 rows, dOut);
+    }
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
